**[P0] Import + verify official roster: Oklahoma (OK)**
- PARENT: c5a813bb-9223-4dc1-95aa-65637eb6940b
- ORIGIN: 2026-07-15, Muxin's direct pick to continue the manual state-by-state track (card 8530a468 / Texas) after AZ + TX. No specific technical rationale like TX's Senate-path gap — OK is simply the next state in sequence. `src/data/states/OK.json` (already in-repo, verified 2026-06-10) confirms OK's 2026 calendar: primary 2026-06-16, primary runoff 2026-08-25, general 2026-11-03. **Note the runoff date is in the future relative to card creation (2026-07-15)** — unlike TX, where both primary and runoff were already decided when that build started. Any OK district whose primary didn't produce an outright winner will not have a determined nominee until after 2026-08-25; the build must NOT guess a runoff outcome before that date (see SAFETY).
- OUTCOME: One more state built and verified end-to-end through the pattern cards 637c2583 (Arizona) and 8530a468 (Texas) proved: official roster governs the candidate set + incumbent-on-ballot status for that state (House AND Senate, if OK has a 2026 Senate contest — confirm during build, not assumed here) only, flag-gated, zero behavior change elsewhere, verified live against an isolated Neon staging branch before any sign-off.
- OUTCOME UPDATE (2026-07-15): Built + verified live. All 5 OK House districts + the Senate race (6 contests total) verified against staging with 0 mismatches — full detail in `docs/operations/oklahoma-vertical-slice-data-check.md`. OK's official source is NOT Civix-vended (a static filing-list PDF + a plain server-rendered results portal, both easier than TX's JS SPA). Added a new `runoff_pending` ballotStatus (no migration needed) plus a "Runoff pending" RepCard tag/CTA for OK's two undetermined nominations (US Senate-Dem, OK-1-Rep), pending OK's Aug 25, 2026 runoff. Independently revalidated against the results portal's own CSV export (Muxin manually downloaded it) — exact match on every candidate/vote/percentage across all 10 congressional primary races. A real, non-obvious incumbency finding: the sitting Senator for the seat on the 2026 ballot is Alan Armstrong (appointed 2026-03-24 after Mullin's resignation), not Mullin — confirmed via senate.gov/Bioguide, never guessed from the card's own framing. Nothing merged yet at build time; flag not enabled anywhere persistent; no production writes. Built on branch `feat/ok-official-roster-vertical-slice`.
- IN SCOPE: (1) **first, check whether Oklahoma's official candidate/election source runs Civix-vended software** (same URL pattern as `goelect.txelections.civixapps.com` — subdomain of `*.civixapps.com`, "IvisCbpUi"/"Civix Election Night Results" page titles) — if so, read `docs/operations/nationwide-congressional-roster-plan.md`'s "Civix portal operational playbook" section FIRST, before rediscovering its mechanics from scratch; (2) fetch + hand-transcribe Oklahoma's official Secretary of State / State Election Board candidate roster (both US House — all OK districts — and the 2026 US Senate race, if one exists) into `scripts/congressional-rosters/ok-official-roster-2026.ts`, importing shared types from `scripts/congressional-rosters/types.ts` (do not duplicate AZ's or TX's local type definitions); (3) register it in `scripts/ingest/official-roster.ts`'s `FIXTURES` map (already array-shaped per state — TX's entry is the reference for a state with both chambers); (4) apply migration 0016 (if not already applied to the target staging branch — it fixes a real Senate-row idempotency bug found during the TX build) + run the importer against the isolated staging Neon branch — never production; (5) add automated tests mirroring `officialRoster.test.ts`'s AZ/TX coverage; (6) end-to-end verification against staging with the flag on, comparing literal app output candidate-by-candidate against the official source; (7) write `docs/operations/oklahoma-vertical-slice-data-check.md` with the full comparison AND a written operational-navigation section (site structure, filter sequence, which signals proved reliable, tooling used) per the plan doc's strengthened standing requirement.
- OUT OF SCOPE: any state other than Oklahoma; production database writes/migrations; enabling `OFFICIAL_ROSTER_ENABLED` anywhere persistent; the automated parser-family fan-out (paused, see epic); expanding to non-congressional (state legislature) races.
- SAFETY: official-source reads only (no Ballotpedia as a primary source — comparison/spot-check only); all writes go to the isolated staging branch, never production; a blocked/unpublished/ambiguous official source stays explicit, never guessed; **any OK district whose runoff hasn't happened yet (post-2026-08-25) must be recorded as an explicit not-yet-determined state, never inferred from primary-round standings** (this is the exact mistake caught and corrected during the TX build — a primary leader is not the nominee if a runoff is still pending); incumbency must be cross-checked against an independent official source (e.g. `house.gov`'s member directory for House, similar for Senate), never guessed from an unreliable in-portal `(I)` marker, and never sourced from this app's own FEC-derived `candidates` table.
- ATTENDED: yes — same contract as 637c2583/8530a468. Build + verify, then STOP and surface to Muxin for sign-off before any merge or flag flip.
- DELIVERABLE REQUIREMENT (Muxin, 2026-07-15, strengthened after TX): The final report/summary MUST state, in full (absolute file paths, untruncated URLs): (a) the full absolute file path to the doc holding the app's output/comparison (mirrors `docs/operations/texas-vertical-slice-data-check.md`), (b) the full absolute file path to the fixture file holding the transcribed data, (c) the exact, full official Oklahoma source URL(s) used, and (d) a written operational-navigation section — see the plan doc's "Standing verification-deliverable requirement" and "Civix portal operational playbook" sections.
- GOAL_CONDITION: `npm run check` passes; the new state's fixture imports idempotently to staging (verify by direct row-count query, not just the importer's self-reported count — see migration 0016's history); an end-to-end check against staging (flag on) shows the app's literal output matching the official roster for every *determined* contest in that state (runoff-pending districts explicitly excluded/flagged, not guessed), with the comparison + operational-navigation writeup for review, including the file-path/source-URL deliverable above.
- STATUS: Done — code merged to main 2026-07-15, per Muxin's explicit sign-off (manual CSV/data verification against the official results portal satisfied, plus sign-off to merge AZ→TX→OK in sequence). Flag NOT enabled anywhere persistent; no production writes; nothing exposed to real users.
- DEPENDS ON: [P0] Import + verify official roster: Texas (TX) (continue the manual state-by-state track; TX is the most recently proven build, including the shared migration-0016 fix and the strengthened deliverable-requirement pattern this card follows)
- DECISION: attended manual gate — same authorized scope as 637c2583/8530a468, extended to one additional state. No production mutation, no flag flip, no fan-out beyond the chosen state.
- GROOMED: ready: pattern proven twice (AZ, TX); Civix-playbook + strengthened deliverable-requirement infrastructure already in place — 2026-07-15

**[P0] Import + verify official roster: Alabama (AL)**
- PARENT: c5a813bb-9223-4dc1-95aa-65637eb6940b
- ORIGIN: 2026-07-15, Muxin's decision after the OK build: rather than hand-picking each next state for a specific technical gap or ease of manual verification (most states still have primaries/runoffs ahead of them anyway, so there's no clean "already fully decided" state to prefer), continue the manual track in **plain alphabetical order** from here — Alabama is first alphabetically among states not yet built through this track (AZ, TX, OK are done). A Wave-2 rehearsal (F03, `scripts/congressional-rosters/f03-source-inventory.ts`, jurisdiction "AL") already inventoried Alabama's official source as a head start: Alabama Secretary of State, `sourceFormat: "html"`, `parserFamily: "html_table"` (NOT Civix), landing page `https://www.sos.alabama.gov/alabama-votes/voter/election-information/2026`. That inventory's `contestScope.electionDates` lists `2026-05-19` (primary), `2026-06-16` (runoff), `2026-08-11`, and `2026-11-03` (general) — the `2026-08-11` date doesn't fit the regular primary/runoff/general pattern and may indicate a separate special congressional election; **confirm what that date actually is during this build, don't assume**. Per the now-standing epic-level requirement (see the epic card's own "STANDING REQUIREMENT" bullet and the plan doc's Oklahoma revision section), this build must check Alabama's own official source for any still-undetermined nomination and apply the existing `runoff_pending` ballotStatus + `SeatChallenger.isRunoffPending` + RepCard CTA pattern if one is found — do not assume Alabama's regular May/June cycle is fully decided without checking.
- OUTCOME: One more state built and verified end-to-end through the pattern cards 637c2583 (Arizona), 8530a468 (Texas), and d9b1ef86 (Oklahoma) proved: official roster governs the candidate set + incumbent-on-ballot status for that state (House AND Senate, if AL has a 2026 Senate contest — confirm during build, not assumed here) only, flag-gated, zero behavior change elsewhere, verified live against an isolated Neon staging branch before any sign-off.
- IN SCOPE: (1) confirm Alabama's official source is what the F03 rehearsal found (HTML, not Civix) before assuming any pattern from AZ/TX/OK applies as-is; (2) fetch + hand-transcribe Alabama's official Secretary of State candidate roster (both US House — all AL districts — and the 2026 US Senate race, if one exists) into `scripts/congressional-rosters/al-official-roster-2026.ts`, importing shared types from `scripts/congressional-rosters/types.ts` (do not duplicate AZ's/TX's/OK's local type definitions); (3) register it in `scripts/ingest/official-roster.ts`'s `FIXTURES` map (already array-shaped per state); (4) apply any needed migration (check `db/schema.ts`/staging's live index definition first — the OK build found no migration was needed for the existing `runoff_pending` status since `ballot_status` is plain text) + run the importer against the isolated staging Neon branch — never production; (5) add automated tests mirroring `officialRoster.test.ts`'s AZ/TX/OK coverage; (6) end-to-end verification against staging with the flag on, comparing literal app output candidate-by-candidate against the official source; (7) write `docs/operations/alabama-vertical-slice-data-check.md` with the full comparison AND a written operational-navigation section per the plan doc's standing requirement; (8) check for any still-undetermined nomination (see ORIGIN note above) and apply `runoff_pending` + the RepCard CTA if found — this is now a standing requirement for every remaining 2026 state, not optional.
- OUT OF SCOPE: any state other than Alabama; production database writes/migrations; enabling `OFFICIAL_ROSTER_ENABLED` anywhere persistent; the automated parser-family fan-out (paused, see epic); expanding to non-congressional (state legislature) races.
- SAFETY: official-source reads only (no Ballotpedia as a primary source — comparison/spot-check only); all writes go to the isolated staging branch, never production; a blocked/unpublished/ambiguous official source stays explicit, never guessed; any AL district/seat whose nomination isn't yet determined must be recorded as `runoff_pending`, never inferred from partial results; incumbency must be cross-checked against an independent official source (e.g. `house.gov`'s member directory for House, `senate.gov`/Bioguide for Senate — the OK build found the app's own framing of "who holds this Senate seat" can be stale, so never skip this cross-check), never guessed from an unreliable in-portal signal, and never sourced from this app's own FEC-derived `candidates` table.
- ATTENDED: build + verify (candidate-by-candidate against the official source, cross-checked against a second independent source when one exists) + self-vet, then MERGE DIRECTLY — per the epic's "STANDING REQUIREMENT — MERGE PROMPTLY, NO SEPARATE SIGN-OFF GATE" (2026-07-15). This supersedes the earlier "STOP for sign-off before merge" contract 637c2583/8530a468/d9b1ef86 carried. Do not let this branch sit unmerged waiting on Muxin — merge as soon as self-vet is clean. The flag flip (`OFFICIAL_ROSTER_ENABLED` going live for real users) is still a separate, gated decision, unaffected by this.
- DELIVERABLE REQUIREMENT (Muxin, 2026-07-15): The final report/summary MUST state, in full (absolute file paths, untruncated URLs): (a) the full absolute file path to the doc holding the app's output/comparison (mirrors `docs/operations/oklahoma-vertical-slice-data-check.md`), (b) the full absolute file path to the fixture file holding the transcribed data, (c) the exact, full official Alabama source URL(s) used, and (d) a written operational-navigation section — see the plan doc's "Standing verification-deliverable requirement" and "Civix portal operational playbook" sections (the latter only applies if Alabama turns out to run Civix after all, which the F03 rehearsal says it doesn't).
- GOAL_CONDITION: `npm run check` passes; the new state's fixture imports idempotently to staging (verify by direct row-count query, not just the importer's self-reported count); an end-to-end check against staging (flag on) shows the app's literal output matching the official roster for every *determined* contest in that state (any undetermined nomination explicitly flagged as `runoff_pending`, not guessed), with the comparison + operational-navigation writeup for review, including the file-path/source-URL deliverable above.
- STATUS: Done
- DEPENDS ON: [P0] Import + verify official roster: Oklahoma (OK) (continue the manual state-by-state track, now in plain alphabetical order per Muxin's 2026-07-15 decision)
- DECISION: self-vet auto-merge gate — same authorized scope as 637c2583/8530a468/d9b1ef86, extended to one additional state. Build + verify + self-vet, then merge directly (no separate Muxin sign-off before merge, per the epic's 2026-07-15 standing requirement). No production mutation, no flag flip, no fan-out beyond the chosen state.
- GROOMED: ready: pattern proven three times (AZ, TX, OK); Civix-playbook + strengthened deliverable-requirement + standing pending-election-tracking infrastructure + merge-promptly policy already in place; Alabama's source already pre-inventoried by the F03 rehearsal — 2026-07-15

Confirm this is still current during the build — the rehearsal is a starting point, not a substitute for a live check.
- OUTCOME: One more jurisdiction built and verified end-to-end through the pattern the AZ, TX, OK, and AL cards proved: official roster governs the candidate set + incumbent-on-ballot status for both US House — all CA districts — and the 2026 US Senate race, if one exists, flag-gated, zero behavior change elsewhere, verified live against an isolated Neon staging branch before merge.
- IN SCOPE: (1) confirm California's official source format/access pattern before assuming any prior state's pattern applies as-is (Civix-vended portal vs. static HTML/PDF/XLSX — check first, per ORIGIN above); (2) fetch + hand-transcribe California's official Secretary of State / State Election Authority candidate roster (both US House — all CA districts — and the 2026 US Senate race, if one exists) into `scripts/congressional-rosters/ca-official-roster-2026.ts`, importing shared types from `scripts/congressional-rosters/types.ts` (do not duplicate any other state's local type definitions); (3) register it in `scripts/ingest/official-roster.ts`'s `FIXTURES` map (already array-shaped per jurisdiction); (4) apply any needed migration (check `db/schema.ts`/staging's live index definition first — no migration has been needed since 0016) + run the importer against the isolated staging Neon branch — never production; (5) add automated tests mirroring `officialRoster.test.ts`'s existing AZ/TX/OK/AL coverage; (6) end-to-end verification against staging with the flag on, comparing literal app output candidate-by-candidate against the official source; (7) write `docs/operations/california-vertical-slice-data-check.md` with the full comparison AND a written operational-navigation section per the plan doc's standing requirement; (8) check for any still-undetermined nomination (a pending runoff, an unfinalized primary, etc.) and apply the existing `runoff_pending` ballotStatus + `SeatChallenger.isRunoffPending` + RepCard CTA pattern if found — this is a standing requirement for every remaining state, not optional.
- OUT OF SCOPE: any jurisdiction other than California; production database writes/migrations; enabling `OFFICIAL_ROSTER_ENABLED` anywhere persistent; the automated parser-family fan-out (paused, see epic — do not confuse this card with the separate "Source inventory" cards on that track); expanding to non-congressional (state legislature) races.
- SAFETY: official-source reads only (no Ballotpedia as a primary source — comparison/spot-check only); all writes go to the isolated staging branch, never production; a blocked/unpublished/ambiguous official source stays explicit, never guessed; any seat whose nomination isn't yet determined must be recorded as `runoff_pending`, never inferred from partial results; incumbency must be cross-checked against an independent official source (e.g. `house.gov`'s member directory for House, `senate.gov`/Bioguide for Senate), never guessed from an unreliable in-portal signal, and never sourced from this app's own FEC-derived `candidates` table.
- ATTENDED: build + verify (candidate-by-candidate against the official source, cross-checked against a second independent source when one exists) + self-vet, then MERGE DIRECTLY — per the epic's "STANDING REQUIREMENT — MERGE PROMPTLY, NO SEPARATE SIGN-OFF GATE" (2026-07-15). Do not let this branch sit unmerged waiting on Muxin — merge as soon as self-vet is clean. The flag flip (`OFFICIAL_ROSTER_ENABLED` going live for real users) is still a separate, gated decision, unaffected by this.
- DELIVERABLE REQUIREMENT (Muxin, 2026-07-15): The final report/summary MUST state, in full (absolute file paths, untruncated URLs): (a) the full absolute file path to the doc holding the app's output/comparison (mirrors `docs/operations/oklahoma-vertical-slice-data-check.md`), (b) the full absolute file path to the fixture file holding the transcribed data, (c) the exact, full official California source URL(s) used, and (d) a written operational-navigation section — see the plan doc's "Standing verification-deliverable requirement" and "Civix portal operational playbook" sections (the latter only applies if California turns out to run Civix).
- GOAL_CONDITION: `npm run check` passes; the new jurisdiction's fixture imports idempotently to staging (verify by direct row-count query, not just the importer's self-reported count); an end-to-end check against staging (flag on) shows the app's literal output matching the official roster for every *determined* contest (any undetermined nomination explicitly flagged as `runoff_pending`, not guessed), with the comparison + operational-navigation writeup for review, including the file-path/source-URL deliverable above.
- STATUS: Done — code merged to main 2026-07-15 (PR #332), flag-gated, no production writes/flag flips
- DECISION: self-vet auto-merge gate — same authorized scope as the AZ/TX/OK/AL cards, extended to one additional jurisdiction. Build + verify + self-vet, then merge directly (no separate Muxin sign-off before merge, per the epic's 2026-07-15 standing requirement). No production mutation, no flag flip, no fan-out beyond the chosen jurisdiction. No `DEPENDS ON` another state card — per Muxin's 2026-07-15 clarification, the manual track scopes each *session* to one state at a time, it does not block the *backlog* to one state at a time; any of these cards may be picked up and built in parallel, each from its own worktree cut from current `origin/main`.
- GROOMED: ready: pattern proven four times (AZ, TX, OK, AL); Civix-playbook + strengthened deliverable-requirement + standing pending-election-tracking infrastructure + merge-promptly policy already in place; F03 rehearsal already inventoried this jurisdiction's source — 2026-07-15

**[P2] Re-check official roster: Mississippi (MS) — after absentee-ballot-availability date**
- PARENT: c5a813bb-9223-4dc1-95aa-65637eb6940b
- ORIGIN: 2026-07-15, opened as part of Mississippi's own vertical-slice build per the epic's "NOT BEFORE DATE-GATE CONVENTION" and "CANDIDATE-WITHDRAWAL DEADLINE TRACKING" standing requirements. MS's fixture (`scripts/congressional-rosters/ms-official-roster-2026.ts`, `docs/operations/mississippi-vertical-slice-data-check.md`) was built at `MS_STAGE = "general"` (post-primary, fully certified March 10, 2026 nominees — every contested federal race cleared an outright majority, so Mississippi's April 7, 2026 runoff was never needed for any congressional seat). **Genuine negative finding, not an omission:** the official 2026 Elections Calendar (`sos.ms.gov/sites/default/files/elections/2026%20Elections%20Calendar.pdf`, the one MS document with a real extractable text layer, unlike the county-recap results PDFs) was searched in full across all 16 pages for "withdraw"/"declin-"/"resign"/"vacanc-"/"removed from the ballot" and carries **no candidate-withdrawal-deadline entry anywhere in the calendar year** — Mississippi's calendar simply does not name one, unlike IN/AK/DE's explicit windows. The closest ballot-related milestones the calendar does name: the General Election Sample Ballot Deadline, September 9, 2026 (Miss. Code Ann. § 23-15-367(3)); and the statutory absentee-ballot-availability date, September 19, 2026 (Miss. Code Ann. § 23-15-715(b)).
- OUTCOME: confirm no nominee recorded in this fixture withdrew, was disqualified, or was otherwise removed/replaced between this build (2026-07-15) and September 19, 2026; since no formal withdrawal-deadline entry exists in MS's own calendar, this re-check's real job is confirming the SoS's candidate-qualifying list / general-ballot roster still matches this fixture as of that date, not verifying against a specific statutory cutoff that doesn't exist.
- IN SCOPE: re-check Mississippi's official source(s) (`sos.ms.gov/content/CandidateQualifying/default.aspx` and `sos.ms.gov/elections-voting/election-results`) for any withdrawal, disqualification, or correction affecting any of MS's 4 US House districts or the US Senate race between build time and September 19, 2026; if a change is found, remove/replace that row per Mississippi's own vacancy-fill procedure (not guessed) and re-verify candidate-by-candidate; re-run the importer against the isolated staging Neon branch if any row changes; update `mississippi-vertical-slice-data-check.md` with the outcome.
- OUT OF SCOPE: any other jurisdiction; production database writes/migrations; enabling `OFFICIAL_ROSTER_ENABLED` anywhere persistent.
- SAFETY: same as the original MS card — official-source reads only, no Ballotpedia as a primary source, never guess a withdrawal/replacement, never source from this app's own FEC-derived `candidates` table.
- GOAL_CONDITION: `npm run check` passes; confirmed no undetected withdrawal/disqualification changed the roster (or the fixture is corrected if one did); deliverable doc updated with the outcome.
- STATUS: Backlog
- NOT BEFORE: 2026-09-19 — Mississippi's statutory absentee-ballot-availability date (Miss. Code Ann. § 23-15-715(b)), the closest official-calendar milestone to a ballot-content-lock date this build could find, given the calendar names no explicit withdrawal deadline (source: `sos.ms.gov/sites/default/files/elections/2026%20Elections%20Calendar.pdf`, confirmed 2026-07-15).
- DECISION: self-vet auto-merge gate, same authorized scope as the original MS card and the epic's standing merge-promptly requirement.
- GROOMED: ready: single clear trigger date, sourced directly from Mississippi's own official election calendar PDF (citing Miss. Code Ann. § 23-15-715(b)); the calendar's lack of a withdrawal-deadline entry is a confirmed negative finding, not an open question — 2026-07-15

**[P0] Import + verify official roster: Nebraska (NE)**
- PARENT: c5a813bb-9223-4dc1-95aa-65637eb6940b
- ORIGIN: 2026-07-15, part of the Phase-1 backlog-population pass that brings every remaining jurisdiction up to build-ready status, per Muxin's decision to keep expanding the manual vertical-slice track (proven for AZ, TX, OK, AL) in parallel rather than one state at a time. No Wave-2/3 rehearsal (F03/I06/I11) covered Nebraska — this build's own research must first establish the official election authority's site, format, and whether it runs Civix-vended software (check the URL pattern against `docs/operations/nationwide-congressional-roster-plan.md`'s "Civix portal operational playbook" before assuming either way), exactly as AZ, TX, and OK each did from a cold start.
- OUTCOME: One more jurisdiction built and verified end-to-end through the pattern the AZ, TX, OK, and AL cards proved: official roster governs the candidate set + incumbent-on-ballot status for both US House — all NE districts — and the 2026 US Senate race, if one exists, flag-gated, zero behavior change elsewhere, verified live against an isolated Neon staging branch before merge.
- IN SCOPE: (1) confirm Nebraska's official source format/access pattern before assuming any prior state's pattern applies as-is (Civix-vended portal vs. static HTML/PDF/XLSX — check first, per ORIGIN above); (2) fetch + hand-transcribe Nebraska's official Secretary of State / State Election Authority candidate roster (both US House — all NE districts — and the 2026 US Senate race, if one exists) into `scripts/congressional-rosters/ne-official-roster-2026.ts`, importing shared types from `scripts/congressional-rosters/types.ts` (do not duplicate any other state's local type definitions); (3) register it in `scripts/ingest/official-roster.ts`'s `FIXTURES` map (already array-shaped per jurisdiction); (4) apply any needed migration (check `db/schema.ts`/staging's live index definition first — no migration has been needed since 0016) + run the importer against the isolated staging Neon branch — never production; (5) add automated tests mirroring `officialRoster.test.ts`'s existing AZ/TX/OK/AL coverage; (6) end-to-end verification against staging with the flag on, comparing literal app output candidate-by-candidate against the official source; (7) write `docs/operations/nebraska-vertical-slice-data-check.md` with the full comparison AND a written operational-navigation section per the plan doc's standing requirement; (8) check for any still-undetermined nomination (a pending runoff, an unfinalized primary, etc.) and apply the existing `runoff_pending` ballotStatus + `SeatChallenger.isRunoffPending` + RepCard CTA pattern if found — this is a standing requirement for every remaining state, not optional.
- OUT OF SCOPE: any jurisdiction other than Nebraska; production database writes/migrations; enabling `OFFICIAL_ROSTER_ENABLED` anywhere persistent; the automated parser-family fan-out (paused, see epic — do not confuse this card with the separate "Source inventory" cards on that track); expanding to non-congressional (state legislature) races.
- SAFETY: official-source reads only (no Ballotpedia as a primary source — comparison/spot-check only); all writes go to the isolated staging branch, never production; a blocked/unpublished/ambiguous official source stays explicit, never guessed; any seat whose nomination isn't yet determined must be recorded as `runoff_pending`, never inferred from partial results; incumbency must be cross-checked against an independent official source (e.g. `house.gov`'s member directory for House, `senate.gov`/Bioguide for Senate), never guessed from an unreliable in-portal signal, and never sourced from this app's own FEC-derived `candidates` table.
- ATTENDED: build + verify (candidate-by-candidate against the official source, cross-checked against a second independent source when one exists) + self-vet, then MERGE DIRECTLY — per the epic's "STANDING REQUIREMENT — MERGE PROMPTLY, NO SEPARATE SIGN-OFF GATE" (2026-07-15). Do not let this branch sit unmerged waiting on Muxin — merge as soon as self-vet is clean. The flag flip (`OFFICIAL_ROSTER_ENABLED` going live for real users) is still a separate, gated decision, unaffected by this.
- DELIVERABLE REQUIREMENT (Muxin, 2026-07-15): The final report/summary MUST state, in full (absolute file paths, untruncated URLs): (a) the full absolute file path to the doc holding the app's output/comparison (mirrors `docs/operations/oklahoma-vertical-slice-data-check.md`), (b) the full absolute file path to the fixture file holding the transcribed data, (c) the exact, full official Nebraska source URL(s) used, and (d) a written operational-navigation section — see the plan doc's "Standing verification-deliverable requirement" and "Civix portal operational playbook" sections (the latter only applies if Nebraska turns out to run Civix).
- GOAL_CONDITION: `npm run check` passes; the new jurisdiction's fixture imports idempotently to staging (verify by direct row-count query, not just the importer's self-reported count); an end-to-end check against staging (flag on) shows the app's literal output matching the official roster for every *determined* contest (any undetermined nomination explicitly flagged as `runoff_pending`, not guessed), with the comparison + operational-navigation writeup for review, including the file-path/source-URL deliverable above.
- STATUS: Done
- DECISION: self-vet auto-merge gate — same authorized scope as the AZ/TX/OK/AL cards, extended to one additional jurisdiction. Build + verify + self-vet, then merge directly (no separate Muxin sign-off before merge, per the epic's 2026-07-15 standing requirement). No production mutation, no flag flip, no fan-out beyond the chosen jurisdiction. - PARENT: c5a813bb-9223-4dc1-95aa-65637eb6940b

- PRECONDITION (fan-in): ALL of I05, I06, I07, I08, I09, I10, I11 must be Done before this card starts. The board parser honors only ONE DEPENDS ON per card (first-wins), so this card names I11 (last roster-a group) but its TRUE blocker is all seven I-groups. Confirm every I05–I11 is Done on the Step-0.5 readiness re-check before starting.
- OUTCOME: One consolidated national inventory proving all 56 jurisdictions are accounted for; every expected 2026 contest maps to an official-source path or evidenced explicit state; parser families and access constraints frozen; non-sensitive public golden addresses selected for later app testing; no placeholder/unknown adapter promoted.
- IN SCOPE: Fan-in of the seven I-group inventories into one validated national record; cross-group reconciliation against the F02 exact-contest oracle; freeze of parser-family classes and access constraints; selection of public golden addresses; EMIT the exact pilot and adapter cards (P16 Texas, P17 Alabama, P18 California, and one P19 card per still-unproven source/semantic class) as new Backlog cards — each born decided (stamp its DECISION from the plan's "Pre-authorized execution decisions" pilots clause: official-source reads only per the NON-NEGOTIABLE SOURCE DECISION, writes only to isolated staging, fail-closed) and pre-GROOMED so the run continues without a drip-feed approval.
- OUT OF SCOPE: Candidate ingestion, schema migration (M13), promotion engine (M14), staging provisioning (M15), the pilots themselves, production mutation, scheduled refreshes.
- SAFETY: Fail-closed if any I-group is incomplete or any jurisdiction lacks an official-source path/evidenced state — never consolidate a partial national inventory; no placeholder/unknown adapter card may be promoted; a filing list or calendar-only source can never be recorded as a qualified/certified roster.
- TESTS: A national consolidation verifier proves all 56 jurisdictions present, every F02 expected contest mapped, no unknown/placeholder adapter, and parser-family/access-constraint freeze internally consistent; it fails if any group is missing.
- GOAL_CONDITION: The national inventory verifier passes for all 56 jurisdictions with no silent omission and no placeholder adapter; the emitted pilot/adapter cards exist as Backlog cards; npm run check passes.

**[P0] M14 — Private artifact abstraction and fail-closed promotion engine (TDD, fakes only)**
- PARENT: c5a813bb-9223-4dc1-95aa-65637eb6940b
- ORIGIN: Wave 4 of the nationwide official-source congressional roster plan (docs/operations/nationwide-congressional-roster-plan.md, "M14 — Private artifact abstraction and fail-closed promotion engine"); pre-groomed 2026-07-14.
- OUTCOME: A private-artifact storage abstraction and a fail-closed promotion engine proven, by TDD against fake Blob/database implementations, to retain the prior promoted snapshot in every failure case.
- IN SCOPE: The artifact-store interface plus a fake implementation; the promotion engine and its state machine; exhaustive failure-case tests (fetch failure, validation failure, partial/blocked coverage, calendar conflict) each proving the previously promoted snapshot is retained.
- OUT OF SCOPE: Real Blob/database wiring; staging/production resources (M15); scheduled ingestion; candidate ingestion; any production mutation.
- SAFETY: Every failure path is fail-closed — a failed acquisition/validation NEVER promotes and NEVER downgrades the last good snapshot; no real external service is contacted; all I/O goes through the fakes.
- TESTS: A TDD suite drives the engine entirely through fake Blob/database implementations; every enumerated failure case asserts prior-snapshot retention and no promotion.
- GOAL_CONDITION: The promotion engine passes its full fail-closed TDD suite against fakes with prior-snapshot retention proven for every failure case; npm run check passes.
- SHIP: auto-pending-merge
- PAUSED (Muxin, 2026-07-15): Superseded by the epic's manual state-by-state pivot — see epic card. Do not resume until Muxin re-authorizes.
- STATUS: To Do

- DECISION: authorized — implementation and TDD against fake Blob/database only; NO real storage/database credentials, NO external service, NO production mutation. Pure code plus tests.
- GROOMED: ready: explicit fake-only TDD scope, fail-closed retention safeguard, and full-failure-case goal condition — 2026-07-14
- LANE: roster-a

**[P0] M15 — Isolated staging wire-up and canary (consumes pre-provisioned secrets)**
- PARENT: c5a813bb-9223-4dc1-95aa-65637eb6940b
- ORIGIN: Wave 4 of the nationwide official-source congressional roster plan (docs/operations/nationwide-congressional-roster-plan.md, "M15 — Isolated staging resources" + "Pre-authorized execution decisions"); pre-groomed 2026-07-14 as an auto wire-up that consumes secrets Muxin provisions out of band.
- PRECONDITION (secrets): Muxin provisions the private Blob store and an isolated Neon staging branch OUT OF BAND and sets `ROSTER_STAGING_BLOB_TOKEN` and `ROSTER_STAGING_DATABASE_URL`. If EITHER secret is absent/empty, this card stops with an honest "staging not provisioned" state — it does NOT provision anything.
- OUTCOME: The roster artifact-store and promotion engine (M14) are wired to the pre-provisioned private Blob + isolated Neon staging branch, and a canary run proves connectivity and fail-closed behavior against staging only.
- IN SCOPE: Reading the two pre-set staging secrets; wiring M14's artifact-store/promotion abstraction to them; a canary that writes/reads a throwaway staging artifact and verifies fail-closed retention; applying M13's additive migration to the ISOLATED staging Neon branch only.
- OUT OF SCOPE: Provisioning or creating any cloud resource; writing/rotating secrets; ANY production database or Blob; pilots; live candidate data.
- SAFETY: Never provisions cloud resources and never writes secrets — it only consumes pre-set ones. Refuses to run against any URL that is not the isolated staging branch; production is never touched. Missing/empty secret → honest stop, never a guess or a provision.
- TESTS: Canary asserts staging connectivity via the two secrets, a round-trip artifact write/read against staging Blob, staging migration applied additively, and fail-closed retention on a simulated failure; no production endpoint is contacted.
- GOAL_CONDITION: Both staging secrets present → the canary proves staging wire-up + additive staging migration + fail-closed retention against staging only, and npm run check passes (Done). If EITHER secret is absent, the card does NOT complete and is NOT marked Done — it surfaces "staging not provisioned — set ROSTER_STAGING_BLOB_TOKEN + ROSTER_STAGING_DATABASE_URL" and holds for Muxin, so nothing downstream (pilots) runs without real staging.
- SHIP: auto-pending-merge
- PAUSED (Muxin, 2026-07-15): Superseded by the epic's manual state-by-state pivot — see epic card. NOTE: `ROSTER_STAGING_DATABASE_URL` was provisioned 2026-07-15 (Vercel Dev+Preview) and is already in active use by the manual track (card 637c2583's AZ migration/import ran against it directly, bypassing M14's fake-only abstraction). `ROSTER_STAGING_BLOB_TOKEN` was not needed/set. Do not resume this card's own wire-up until M13/M14 are re-authorized.
- STATUS: To Do
- DEPENDS ON: M14 — Private artifact abstraction and fail-closed promotion engine
- DECISION: authorized (pre-authorized per the plan's "Pre-authorized execution decisions", M15 clause) — CONSUMES pre-provisioned staging secrets and wires/canaries staging only; NEVER provisions cloud resources, NEVER writes secrets, NEVER touches production; missing secret → honest stop. Muxin sets `ROSTER_STAGING_BLOB_TOKEN` + `ROSTER_STAGING_DATABASE_URL` out of band.

**[P1] Bump Next.js 15.5.12 -> 15.5.20+ (patches 6 high-severity CVEs)**
- npm audit --omit=dev flags 6 high-severity Next.js advisories at installed 15.5.12: connection-exhaustion DoS in Cache Components (GHSA-mg66-mrh9-m8jx), Image Optimization API DoS (GHSA-h64f-5h5j-jqjh), WebSocket-upgrade SSRF (GHSA-c4j6-fc7j-m34r), dynamic-route-param middleware/proxy bypass (GHSA-492v-c6pp-mqqv), RSC cache poisoning (GHSA-wfc6-r584-vfw7), segment-prefetch + i18n middleware bypass (GHSA-267c-6grr-h53f, GHSA-36qx-fr4f-26g5).
- All 7 advisories list first_patched_version 15.5.16 (confirmed via GitHub Advisory API); 15.5.20 is the latest 15.x release available (dist-tag 'backport') -- a same-major patch bump, no v16 migration needed.
- ORIGIN: idea-scout 2026-07-14 -- dependency-security-advisory
- NEEDS DECISION: machine-invented candidate; explicit human promotion required
- EVIDENCE: https://github.com/advisories/GHSA-mg66-mrh9-m8jx (+6 sibling GHSA ids); local `npm audit --omit=dev` output (retrieved 2026-07-14)
- HYPOTHESIS: same-major patch bump (15.5.12 -> 15.5.20) should be low-risk and closes all 6 flagged CVEs; uncertainty -- unverified whether the 15.5.13-15.5.20 patch range carries any breaking change for this app's usage (Cache Components / middleware / i18n); confirm with a full build+test pass before merge.
- GOAL_CONDITION: npm audit --omit=dev reports zero Next.js advisories after the bump; npm run build and the full test suite pass at the new pinned version.
- STATUS: To Do
- DECISION: PROMOTED by Muxin 2026-07-16 (approved as part of the ship-runway housekeeping batch). Standard self-vet auto-merge once build + full test suite pass at the pinned version.

**[idea] Evaluate whether ballot chat should hard-redirect election-logistics questions instead of answering via LLM**
- Current chat surface (docs/BALLOT_PROMPT.md / theme-extraction) is scoped to issue-alignment conversation; it isn't confirmed whether the prompt explicitly refuses/redirects a voter who asks a logistics question ("where do I vote", "when does registration close") to an official source rather than answering from the model's own knowledge.
- External signal: a 2026 Demos/Electoral Commission evaluation found AI chatbots gave inaccurate answers on ~34% of election-procedural test questions in a Scottish-election study; bipartisan US lawmakers and the NY Attorney General have since publicly warned against relying on chatbots for voting/election-logistics answers ahead of the 2026 midterms.
- ORIGIN: idea-scout 2026-07-14 -- external-research/regulatory-industry-signal
- NEEDS DECISION: machine-invented candidate; explicit human promotion required
- EVIDENCE: https://www.resultsense.com/news/2026-05-21-demos-electoral-commission-ai-chatbots-scottish-election/ ; https://ag.ny.gov/publications/protecting-new-york-voters-ai-generated-election-misinformation (retrieved 2026-07-14)
- HYPOTHESIS: a small, bounded prompt/guard change routing logistics questions to the app's own Google-Civic-backed data (or an explicit official-source redirect) would close a plausible-but-unverified gap; uncertainty -- not yet confirmed the current prompt actually mishandles a logistics question in practice, and product scope may already exclude this by design; a manual eval should run before any code change.
- GOAL_CONDITION: a short manual eval (N sample logistics questions run through the live chat) either confirms current behavior is already safe (card closes as not-needed) or a prompt change ships that verifiably redirects logistics questions rather than answering them from the model's own general knowledge.
- DECIDED (Muxin, 2026-07-16): NOT a hard redirect. Her ruling verbatim: "we do our best attempt but always say, to be absolutely sure check these resources. Because ppl just want a general sense of when and where, at least I think so, for planning's sake." So: chat MAY give a best-attempt general answer to logistics questions (when/where, for planning), but every such answer MUST end with an explicit to-be-absolutely-sure pointer to official resources (the state election authority / the app's own Google-Civic-backed polling data where available). The eval in GOAL_CONDITION still runs first to check what current behavior is; the prompt change then implements best-attempt-plus-mandatory-official-pointer, not a refusal.
- STATUS: To Do
- DECISION: decided 2026-07-16 (best-attempt + mandatory official-source pointer; no hard redirect). Self-vet auto-merge; prompt-only change.

**[P2] Re-check official roster: Arizona (AZ) — after primary certification**
- PARENT: c5a813bb-9223-4dc1-95aa-65637eb6940b
- ORIGIN: 2026-07-15, part of a retroactive governing-calendar-dates sweep across every completed manual-track state (AL/AK/AZ/OK/TX/CO/CA/AR), run after the CO build surfaced that no state's card previously required recording or scheduling a re-check date (see the new "NOT BEFORE DATE-GATE CONVENTION" and plan doc item (e) above/nearby). AZ's fixture (`scripts/congressional-rosters/az-official-roster-2026.ts`, `docs/operations/arizona-vertical-slice-data-check.md`) was built at `AZ_STAGE = "primary"` against the official candidate-nominations PDF — Arizona's 2026 primary (July 21, 2026, moved this cycle from the historical "first Tuesday in August" by a Feb 2026 law signed by Gov. Hobbs) was still upcoming at build time, so the fixture reflects primary-stage filers, not general-ballot nominees.
- OUTCOME: AZ's fixture is re-derived from the certified primary results to reflect real general-ballot nominees (not primary-stage candidate lists) for all AZ US House districts + the Senate race.
- IN SCOPE: re-verify AZ's official source (azsos.gov) post-certification; update `az-official-roster-2026.ts` entries' `ballotStatus`/`stage` from primary-stage to determined general-ballot nominees (or `runoff_pending` if AZ has a runoff mechanism and any race needs it — confirm, don't assume); re-run the importer against the isolated staging Neon branch; update `arizona-vertical-slice-data-check.md` with the new candidate-by-candidate comparison; also check Arizona's official candidate-withdrawal deadline/window (azsos.gov's write-in/withdrawn candidate list already covers withdrawals up to 2026-06-02 pre-primary — confirm whether AZ has a separate POST-primary withdrawal window before the general too, and record it per the epic's withdrawal-deadline standing requirement); record any NEW governing calendar dates found (per plan doc item (e)) and open a further `NOT BEFORE` follow-up card if anything is still undetermined.

Louisiana's US SENATE contest (Cassidy's Class 2 seat) IS fully determined already — closed primary (May 16) + runoff (June 27) both already past — and is NOT in scope for this follow-up card.

R.S. 18:1400.1 per usvotefoundation.org, confirmed 2026-07-15; compute the exact date once the House field and any resulting runoff status are known); update `louisiana-vertical-slice-data-check.md` with the new candidate-by-candidate comparison; record any NEW governing calendar dates found and open a further `NOT BEFORE` follow-up card if the Dec 12 runoff leaves anything undetermined.

It exists because Utah Code § 20A-9-202(6) permits a candidate to withdraw by filing a written affidavit with the clerk without itself stating a standalone numeric deadline distinct from the ballot-printing timeline — the practical, functional lock point is Utah's own election calendar's September 16, 2026 UOCAVA ballot-transmission deadline (ballots must be finalized/printed by then). Utah's 2026 congressional map is also a genuinely novel case among states built so far — a court-ordered mid-decade redistricting moved 3 of 4 sitting US Representatives (Moore, Maloy, Kennedy) into a different district number than the one they currently hold; this re-check should reconfirm none of the 19 rows changed, not just check for a withdrawal.
- OUTCOME: UT's fixture is re-verified against the official source after the ballot-content lock point, confirming no late withdrawal occurred and no candidate's status changed, or updated to reflect whichever did.
- IN SCOPE: re-verify Utah's official source (`vote.utah.gov/2026-candidate-filings/`) after September 16, 2026, for any status change among the 19 rows (a withdrawal would newly appear as a `Withdrew` status on the filing table); re-run the importer against the isolated staging Neon branch if any change is found; update `utah-vertical-slice-data-check.md` with the re-check result either way (a confirmed "no change" is still a required update, not a no-op); also confirm no write-in candidate declared by the August 1, 2026 write-in deadline (per the epic's withdrawal/write-in standing requirement) needs to be added.
- OUT OF SCOPE: any other jurisdiction; production database writes/migrations; enabling `OFFICIAL_ROSTER_ENABLED` anywhere persistent.
- SAFETY: same as the original UT card — official-source reads only, no Ballotpedia as a primary source, never guess an undetermined nomination, cross-check incumbency independently (house.gov, matched by name not old district number given the redistricting, never this app's own FEC-derived `candidates` table).
- GOAL_CONDITION: `npm run check` passes; UT's fixture reflects the final post-deadline roster (confirmed unchanged, or updated with any withdrawal/write-in change); end-to-end verification against staging with the flag on shows correct candidate-by-candidate match; deliverable doc updated with the re-check result and any new governing dates.
- STATUS: Backlog
- NOT BEFORE: 2026-09-17 — the day after Utah's September 16, 2026 UOCAVA ballot-transmission deadline (Utah Code, per the 2026 Utah Election Calendar for Voters & Candidates), the practical point by which Utah's general-ballot candidate slate must be finalized for printing; used here as the functional withdrawal-deadline substitute since Utah Code § 20A-9-202(6) does not itself state a standalone numeric withdrawal deadline (source: `vote.utah.gov/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/2026-Utah-Election-Calendar.pdf`, confirmed 2026-07-16).
- DECISION: self-vet auto-merge gate, same authorized scope as the original UT card and the epic's standing merge-promptly requirement.
- GROOMED: ready: single clear trigger date, sourced directly from Utah's own official election calendar, no open questions — 2026-07-16
